{"lang_code":"en","pos":"name","senses":[{"glosses":["The observation that, if a, b, and c are the lengths of the sides of a triangle with area ƒ, and A, B, and C are the lengths of the sides of another triangle with area F, then A²(b²+c²-a²)+B²(a²+c²-b²)+C²(a²+b²-c²)≥16Ff,, with equality if and only if the two triangles are similar with pairs of corresponding sides (A, a), (B, b), and (C, c)."],"synonyms":[{"word":"Neuberg-Pedoe inequality"}],"topics":["geometry","mathematics","sciences"]}],"upstream_line":10905550,"upstream_offset":26178421868,"upstream_record_sha256":"367ab635f9c4820f6b7ef18e98d273e3c83a40d01f82ce176cb27ff5bb0c7cde","word":"Pedoe's inequality"}
